output.md
I've added the log sheet's logic: the per-activity field table, `CardioLogDraft` (parsing, range checks, derived pace and speed, the request) and the 14 tests. All 14 pass and ktlint and detekt are clean, but **the commit didn't happen**: git has no author name or email set in this environment, and I didn't want to set one for you. The changes are staged under the planned commit message.

**Files**
- `CardioActivity.kt`: adds `logFields`, which reads from the table below.
- **`CardioLogField.kt`** — the plan names this file `CardioLogFields.kt`, but ktlint refuses that name because the file holds the single class `CardioLogField`. Correction for the record: `**Files:**` entry `…/cardio/CardioLogFields.kt` → `…/cardio/CardioLogField.kt`. It holds the enum and the field order per activity, as listed in Step 1.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt`: the data class with the API the plan specifies. It also takes `activity`, `units`, `texts` and `left` as constructor parameters, plus `fresh`, `of` and `durationText`.
- `CardioLogChecks.kt` (allowed collateral, split out for detekt): duration and pace parsing, and the range checks with their messages.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt`: the 14 named tests.

**Commands and results**
1. `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` before the code existed: RED, failed to compile (`Unresolved reference 'CardioLogDraft'`).
2. Same command after writing the code: GREEN, 14 tests, 0 failures.
3. Checking that the tests catch breakage: I changed the request to send the derived pace and set the rower's cadence minimum to 1. Result: "14 tests completed, 4 failed", `BUILD FAILED`. I restored both files from backups. I didn't record which four tests failed, because the next build overwrote the XML report.
4. `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat`: the first run failed and the second was clean. The run after that reported two problems: the file-name rule above and detekt's "destructuring with 4 entries". I fixed both.
5. `./gradlew :shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests 'CardioLogDraftTest'`: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 14 tests, 0 failures.
6. `git commit`: failed with `Author identity unknown`.

**Not checked: the backend repo isn't in this environment.** I couldn't compare `logFields` with the backend's `CardioActivity.kt` or the ranges with `CardioLogSpecification.kt`. The field orders and ranges come from the plan's text only.

**Wording I chose that the plan doesn't give:**
- **Distance:** "between 0 and 1000 km" (621 mi; 1000000 m for the rower).
- **Pace:** "between 1:00 and 60:00 /km" (10:00 /500 m for the rower).
- **Duration out of range:** "between 0:01 and 24:00:00 h:mm:ss".
- **Speed:** uses the same 0.1–120 range whether the workout is in km/h or mph.
- **Non-numeric text in a number field:** shows that field's range message.

**Test values I made up, because the frame figures aren't here:**
- **Rower (L4):** 30:00 over 7120 m, which gives "2:06".
- **Saved log:** the values in `aSavedLogFillsTheDraft`.

Please confirm the wording and these figures against the design.

**Your call:** set a git identity (or tell me which to use) and I'll make the commit.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+private const val MINUTE = 60
+private const val SIXTY = 60
+private const val DURATION_MAX = 86_400
+private const val HR_MIN = 30
+private const val HR_MAX = 250
+private const val TOTAL_MAX = 20_000
+private const val PACE_MIN = 60
+private const val PACE_MAX = 3_600
+private const val ROWER_PACE_MAX = 600
+private const val SPEED_MIN = 0.1
+private const val SPEED_MAX = 120.0
+private const val CADENCE_MAX = 250
+private const val ROWER_CADENCE_MIN = 10
+private const val ROWER_CADENCE_MAX = 60
+private const val POWER_MAX = 2_500
+private const val DISTANCE_MAX_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+
+private const val DURATION_SHAPE = "use h:mm:ss"
+private const val MAX_BELOW_AVG = "max heart rate is below average"
+
+private val clockShape = Regex("""(?:(\d+):([0-5]\d)|(\d+)):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+/** `h:mm:ss` or `m:ss`, minutes and seconds 00–59 after a colon (KAN-939 `duration-input`); else null. */
+internal fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val groups = clockShape.matchEntire(text.trim())?.groupValues ?: return null
+    val minutes = if (groups[1].isNotEmpty()) groups[1].toInt() * SIXTY + groups[2].toInt() else groups[3].toInt()
+    return minutes * MINUTE + groups[4].toInt()
+}
+
+/** A pace is `m:ss` only. */
+internal fun parsePace(text: String): Int? = text.takeIf { it.count { c -> c == ':' } == 1 }?.let(::parseClock)
+
+/**
+ * The backend's ranges (`CardioLogSpecification`), rower cadence narrowed to L4's 10–60 (KAN-939
+ * `client-ranges`); an empty field is never in error.
+ */
+internal fun logErrors(draft: CardioLogDraft): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+    val errors = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+    val fields = draft.activity.logFields
+
+    fun check(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        message: String?,
+    ) {
+        if (field in fields && draft.typed(field) != null && message != null) errors[field] = message
+    }
+    check(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationError(draft))
+    check(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, distanceError(draft))
+    check(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, paceError(draft))
+    check(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, decimalError(draft.typedSpeed(), SPEED_MIN, SPEED_MAX, speedUnit(draft.units)))
+    check(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, 0, TOTAL_MAX, elevationUnit(draft.units)))
+    check(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, HR_MIN, HR_MAX, "bpm"))
+    check(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, maxHeartRateError(draft))
+    check(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, cadenceError(draft))
+    check(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, 1, POWER_MAX, "W"))
+    check(CardioLogField.CALORIES, wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.CALORIES, 0, TOTAL_MAX, "kcal"))
+    return errors
+}
+
+private fun between(
+    min: Any,
+    max: Any,
+    unit: String,
+) = "between $min and $max $unit"
+
+private fun durationError(draft: CardioLogDraft): String? {
+    val seconds = draft.durationSeconds() ?: return DURATION_SHAPE
+    return if (seconds in 1..DURATION_MAX) null else between("0:01", "24:00:00", "h:mm:ss")
+}
+
+private fun distanceError(draft: CardioLogDraft): String? {
+    val unit = draft.distanceUnit
+    val max = (DISTANCE_MAX_METRES / unit.metres).toLong()
+    val distance = draft.distance()
+    val inRange = distance != null && distance.value > 0 && distance.value * unit.metres < DISTANCE_MAX_METRES
+    return if (inRange) null else between(0, max, unit.name.lowercase())
+}
+
+private fun paceError(draft: CardioLogDraft): String? {
+    val max = if (draft.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) ROWER_PACE_MAX else PACE_MAX
+    val unit = CardioMetric.PACE.unitLabel(draft.activity, draft.units).orEmpty()
+    val pace = draft.typedPace()
+    return if (pace != null && pace in PACE_MIN..max) null else between(durationText(PACE_MIN), durationText(max), unit)
+}
+
+private fun cadenceError(draft: CardioLogDraft): String? {
+    val rower = draft.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    val unit = CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(draft.activity, draft.units).orEmpty()
+    return if (rower) {
+        wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, ROWER_CADENCE_MIN, ROWER_CADENCE_MAX, unit)
+    } else {
+        wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, 1, CADENCE_MAX, unit)
+    }
+}
+
+private fun maxHeartRateError(draft: CardioLogDraft): String? {
+    wholeError(draft, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, HR_MIN, HR_MAX, "bpm")?.let { return it }
+    val avg = draft.whole(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+    val max = draft.whole(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+    return if (avg != null && max != null && max < avg) MAX_BELOW_AVG else null
+}
+
+private fun wholeError(
+    draft: CardioLogDraft,
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    min: Int,
+    max: Int,
+    unit: String,
+): String? = draft.whole(field).let { if (it != null && it in min..max) null else between(min, max, unit) }
+
+private fun decimalError(
+    value: Double?,
+    min: Double,
+    max: Double,
+    unit: String,
+): String? = if (value != null && value in min..max) null else between(min, max, unit)
+
+private fun speedUnit(units: DistanceUnit) = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+
+private fun elevationUnit(units: DistanceUnit) = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's values as typed (KAN-939 L1–L6): [texts] per field, [effort], the workout's [note], and the
+ * fields the user has [left] — an error shows only on those (`client-ranges`). A blank pace or speed is
+ * [derived] for show and never sent (`derived-never-sent`).
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> = logErrors(this)
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = durationSeconds() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(durationSeconds(), distance(), typedPace(), activity, units)?.let(::clock)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(durationSeconds(), distance(), typedSpeed(), units)?.toString()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = durationSeconds() ?: 0,
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = typedPace(),
+            avgSpeed = typedSpeed(),
+            avgHeartRate = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = whole(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = whole(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = whole(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    internal fun typed(field: CardioLogField): String? = texts[field]?.trim()?.takeIf { field in activity.logFields && it.isNotEmpty() }
+
+    internal fun durationSeconds(): Int? = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION)?.let(::parseClock)
+
+    internal fun typedPace(): Int? = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)?.let(::parsePace)
+
+    internal fun typedSpeed(): Double? = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED)?.toDoubleOrNull()
+
+    internal fun whole(field: CardioLogField): Int? = typed(field)?.toIntOrNull()
+
+    /** The rower's distance is metres whatever the workout's unit (KAN-936 `rower-is-metric`). */
+    internal val distanceUnit: DistanceUnit get() = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    internal fun distance(): CardioDistance? = typed(CardioLogField.DISTANCE)?.toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit) }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** A new log; Duration from the clock, floored to the second, when the workout is in progress (L5). */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val started = workout.startedAt?.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+            val texts = started?.let { mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText((now - it).inWholeSeconds.toInt())) }
+            return CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, texts.orEmpty(), note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+        }
+
+        /** A saved log back as text; a derived pace or speed stays blank, so [derived] recomputes it. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val blank = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return blank
+            val texts =
+                mapOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE to log.distance?.let { number(it.value * it.unit.metres / blank.distanceUnit.metres) },
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to log.avgPaceSeconds?.let(::clock),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED to log.avgSpeed?.let(::number),
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to log.elevationGain?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to log.avgHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to log.maxHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to log.avgCadence?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to log.avgPower?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES to log.calories?.toString(),
+                ).mapNotNull { (field, text) -> text?.let { field to it } }.toMap()
+            return blank.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up (`41:06`, `1:12:40`). */
+internal fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/** A field of the log sheet (KAN-939 `log-fields-table`); [CardioActivity.logFields] orders them per sheet. */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val tail = listOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES, CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE)
+private val heartRate = listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+
+internal fun cardioLogFields(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> {
+    val head = listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE)
+    return when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            head + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) + heartRate +
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + tail
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            head + CardioLogField.AVG_PACE + heartRate + tail
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            head + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) + heartRate +
+                listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + tail
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            head + CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED + heartRate + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + tail
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            head + heartRate + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + tail
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            head + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + heartRate + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + tail
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Easy run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        vararg typed: Pair<CardioLogField, String>,
+    ) = typed.fold(CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity), now)) { d, (field, value) -> d.with(field, value) }
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val run = listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        val ride =
+            listOf(
+                DURATION,
+                DISTANCE,
+                AVG_SPEED,
+                ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                AVG_CADENCE,
+                AVG_POWER,
+                CALORIES,
+                EFFORT,
+                NOTE,
+            )
+        assertEquals(run, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride - ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(run - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE, CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "1:12:40")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach {
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to it)).errors()[DURATION], it)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val empty = draft()
+        assertTrue(empty.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertFalse(empty.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(empty.with(DURATION, "33:12").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02"))
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02", AVG_PACE to "6:30"))
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE, DURATION to "1:12:40", DISTANCE to "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, DURATION to "30:00", DISTANCE to "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(AVG_HEART_RATE to "148", MAX_HEART_RATE to "139"))
+        assertEquals(mapOf(MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average"), d.errors())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, AVG_CADENCE to "8").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, AVG_CADENCE to "8")
+        assertTrue(AVG_CADENCE in d.errors())
+        assertNull(d.shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.left(AVG_CADENCE).shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00.000Z"))
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(started, now).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), now).text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 139,
+                maxHeartRate = 168,
+                calories = 512,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 24,
+                avgCadence = 172,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED, log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", d.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("139", d.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("168", d.text(MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("512", d.text(CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", d.text(ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("172", d.text(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("", d.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(6, d.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02", AVG_HEART_RATE to "148")).copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 148,
+                effort = 6,
+            ),
+            d.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}

- **Range message units:**

  | Field | Message |
  |---|---|
  | Duration | `between 0:01 and 24:00:00` |
  | Pace | `between 1:00 and 60:00 /km` (rower: `10:00 /500 m`) |
  | Distance | `between 0 and 1000 km` (or `621 mi`, or `1000000 m` for the rower) |
  | Heart rate | `bpm` |
  | Calories | `kcal` |
  | Elevation | `m` or `ft` by the workout's unit; the 0–20000 range is applied in that unit |
  | Power | `W` |
  | Cadence | `rpm` on rides, `spm` otherwise |

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val MINUTE = 60
+private const val MAX_DURATION = 86_400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+private val MINUTES = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d)$""")
+private val HOURS = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d)$""")
+
+/** Decision `duration-input`: `h:mm:ss` or `m:ss`, minutes and seconds 00–59 after a colon. */
+internal fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val t = text.trim()
+    HOURS.find(t)?.destructured?.let { (h, m, s) -> return h.toInt() * HOUR + m.toInt() * MINUTE + s.toInt() }
+    return parsePace(t)
+}
+
+/** A pace is `m:ss`. */
+internal fun parsePace(text: String): Int? {
+    val (m, s) = MINUTES.find(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return m.toInt() * MINUTE + s.toInt()
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+internal fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+/** The unit a distance is typed in: metres on the rower whatever the workout's unit (`rower-is-metric`). */
+internal fun distanceUnitOf(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): DistanceUnit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+/** One typed field's error under decision `client-ranges` (the backend's `CardioLogSpecification`, rower cadence 10–60). */
+@Suppress("CyclomaticComplexMethod")
+internal fun fieldError(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    text: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    val imperial = units == DistanceUnit.MI
+    return when (field) {
+        CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+            val s = parseDuration(text) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+            if (s in 1..MAX_DURATION) null else "between 0:01 and 24:00:00"
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+            val s = parsePace(text) ?: return "use m:ss"
+            val max = if (rower) 600 else 3600
+            val basis =
+                if (rower) {
+                    "/500 m"
+                } else if (imperial) {
+                    "/mi"
+                } else {
+                    "/km"
+                }
+            if (s in MINUTE..max) null else "between ${clock(MINUTE)} and ${clock(max)} $basis"
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+            val unit = distanceUnitOf(activity, units)
+            val v = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return NOT_A_NUMBER
+            val max = (MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / unit.metres).toLong()
+            if (v > 0 && v * unit.metres < MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) null else "between 0 and $max ${unit.name.lowercase()}"
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+            val v = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return NOT_A_NUMBER
+            if (v in 0.1..120.0) null else "between 0.1 and 120.0 " + if (imperial) "mph" else "km/h"
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+            intRange(text, 30, 250, "bpm")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+            intRange(text, 0, 20_000, "kcal")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+            intRange(text, 0, 20_000, if (imperial) "ft" else "m")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+            intRange(text, 1, 2500, "W")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+            if (rower) intRange(text, 10, 60, "spm") else intRange(text, 1, 250, if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+            null
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+internal const val NOT_A_NUMBER = "enter a number"
+
+private fun intRange(
+    text: String,
+    min: Int,
+    max: Int,
+    unit: String,
+): String? {
+    val v = text.trim().toIntOrNull() ?: return NOT_A_NUMBER
+    return if (v in min..max) null else "between $min and $max $unit"
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val HUNDRED = 100.0
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (KAN-939 L1–L6): what is typed per field, the effort, the workout's note and
+ * which fields the user has left. Pure — the sheet debounces (decision `client-ranges`).
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** An empty field is never in error; max below avg is MAX_HEART_RATE's. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val errors =
+            activity.logFields
+                .filter { text(it).isNotBlank() }
+                .mapNotNull { f -> fieldError(f, text(f), activity, units)?.let { f to it } }
+                .toMap()
+        val avg = int(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+        val max = int(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        val below = CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE !in errors && avg != null && max != null && max < avg
+        return if (below) errors + (CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average") else errors
+    }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /** Decision `derived-never-sent`: pace or speed from duration and distance while its field is blank. */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        val seconds = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION, ::parseDuration)
+        val distance = distance()
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(seconds, distance, null, activity, units)?.let(::clock)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(seconds, distance, null, units)?.let {
+                    CardioFormat.speed(it, units).substringBefore(' ')
+                }
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort only: never the note (the workout's), never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION, ::parseDuration) ?: 0,
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, ::parsePace),
+            avgSpeed = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED) { it.trim().toDoubleOrNull() },
+            avgHeartRate = int(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = int(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = int(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = int(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = int(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = int(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    private fun <T> typed(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        parse: (String) -> T?,
+    ): T? = if (field in activity.logFields) text(field).takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let(parse) else null
+
+    private fun int(field: CardioLogField): Int? = typed(field) { it.trim().toIntOrNull() }
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? =
+        typed(CardioLogField.DISTANCE) { it.trim().toDoubleOrNull() }?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnitOf(activity, units)) }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** Decision `clock-from-started-at`: Duration prefills with whole seconds since `startedAt` when in progress. */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val started = workout.startedAt?.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+            val texts =
+                started
+                    ?.let {
+                        mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText((now - it).inWholeSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0).toInt()))
+                    }.orEmpty()
+            return CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, texts, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+        }
+
+        /** A saved log back as text; a derived pace or speed is left blank so [derived] recomputes it. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log ?: return CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val unit = distanceUnitOf(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit)
+            val texts =
+                listOfNotNull(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    log.distance?.let { CardioLogField.DISTANCE to number(round2(it.value * it.unit.metres / unit.metres)) },
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to clock(it) },
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED to number(it) },
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "$it" },
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "$it" },
+                    log.calories?.let { CardioLogField.CALORIES to "$it" },
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to "$it" },
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to "$it" },
+                    log.avgPower?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to "$it" },
+                ).toMap()
+            return CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, texts, log.effort, workout.notes.orEmpty())
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun round2(value: Double) = (value * HUNDRED).roundToLong() / HUNDRED

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/** One field of the log sheet (KAN-939 L1–L6); which ones an activity logs is [CardioActivity.logFields]. */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val HEART = listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+private val TAIL = listOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES, CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE)
+
+private val RUN =
+    listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) +
+        HEART + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + TAIL
+
+private val RIDE =
+    listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) +
+        HEART + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + CardioLogField.AVG_POWER + TAIL
+
+/** Decision `log-fields-table`: L1 the outdoor run, L3 the outdoor ride, L4 the rower; the undrawn three drop from them. */
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            RUN
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            RUN - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN - CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            RIDE
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            RIDE - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE) + HEART + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + TAIL
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) +
+                HEART + CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE + TAIL
+        }
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        vararg typed: Pair<CardioLogField, String>,
+    ) = typed.fold(CardioLogDraft.of(workout(activity))) { d, (f, v) -> d.with(f, v) }
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val run = listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        val ride =
+            listOf(
+                DURATION,
+                DISTANCE,
+                AVG_SPEED,
+                ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                AVG_CADENCE,
+                AVG_POWER,
+                CALORIES,
+                EFFORT,
+                NOTE,
+            )
+        assertEquals(run, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(run - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE, CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride - ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "1:12:40")).toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach {
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to it)).errors()[DURATION], it)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val empty = draft(typed = arrayOf(DISTANCE to "5.02"))
+        assertTrue(empty.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertFalse(empty.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(empty.with(DURATION, "33:12").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02"))
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d = draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02", AVG_PACE to "6:30"))
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("6:30", d.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE, DURATION to "1:12:40", DISTANCE to "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, DURATION to "30:00", DISTANCE to "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val errors = draft(typed = arrayOf(AVG_HEART_RATE to "148", MAX_HEART_RATE to "139")).errors()
+        assertEquals(mapOf(MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average"), errors)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, AVG_CADENCE to "8").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertNull(draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, AVG_CADENCE to "8").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, AVG_CADENCE to "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertNull(d.shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.left(AVG_CADENCE).shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val w = workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00.000Z"))
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(w, Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")).text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 139,
+                maxHeartRate = 162,
+                calories = 410,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 24,
+                avgCadence = 172,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED, log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", d.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", d.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("139", d.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("162", d.text(MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("410", d.text(CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", d.text(ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("172", d.text(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("6", d.text(EFFORT))
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val d =
+            draft(typed = arrayOf(DURATION to "33:12", DISTANCE to "5.02", AVG_HEART_RATE to "148", AVG_POWER to "200"))
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(durationSeconds = 1992, distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), avgHeartRate = 148, effort = 6),
+            d.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}

**What changed** (in `shared/.../cardio/`)
- **`CardioActivity.kt`**: added `logFields`, which lists each activity's log fields in the order the plan gives.
- **`CardioLogField.kt`**: the `CardioLogField` enum and the field table for each activity.
- **`CardioLogChecks.kt`** (extra file, allowed for size): parses durations (`h:mm:ss` or `m:ss`) and paces (`m:ss`), formats them, and checks each field's range. Errors read *use h:mm:ss* or *between X and Y unit*, with rower cadence limited to 10–60.
- **`CardioLogDraft.kt`**: an immutable data class with the API the tests use. A blank pace or speed is calculated from duration and distance and never sent. A rower's distance is always in metres. `fresh` fills Duration from `startedAt` (whole seconds, rounded down), and `of` rebuilds the draft from a saved log.
- **Tests:** the fourteen named tests in `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` (before 0, after 14).

**Commands run**
- RED: `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` failed to compile (`Unresolved reference 'CardioLogField'`).

**Questions for you** (I implemented these but didn't treat them as settled)
1. **Messages the plan doesn't give:** I wrote these myself:
   - *use m:ss* for a pace of the wrong shape.
   - *use a number* or *use a whole number* for text that isn't a number.
   - *between 1 and 86400 s* for duration.
   - *between 1:00 and 60:00 /km* for pace.
   - *between 0 and 621.3 mi* for a distance limit converted to miles.
2. **Speed and elevation limits in miles:** I applied the same numbers (0.1–120, 0–20000) to mph and feet, assuming the backend checks the value as typed.
3. **Effort:** an effort typed through `with(EFFORT, …)` isn't range-checked; it's simply ignored if it isn't a number.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION = 86_400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+private const val TEN = 10.0
+private const val MIN_PACE = 60
+private const val ROWER_MAX_PACE = 600
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private val CLOCK = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d)(?::([0-5]\d))?$""")
+private val PACE = Regex("""^(\d+):([0-5]\d)$""")
+
+/** Design `duration-input`: `h:mm:ss` or `m:ss`, whole seconds; null for any other shape. */
+internal fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val (a, b, c) = CLOCK.find(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return if (c.isEmpty()) a.toInt() * 60 + b.toInt() else a.toInt() * HOUR + b.toInt() * 60 + c.toInt()
+}
+
+/** A pace input, `m:ss`. */
+internal fun parsePace(text: String): Int? {
+    val (m, s) = PACE.find(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return m.toInt() * 60 + s.toInt()
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+internal fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+/** One decimal, as the sheet draws a derived speed ("31.9"). */
+internal fun speedText(value: Double): String = CardioFormat.speed(value, DistanceUnit.KM).substringBefore(' ')
+
+/**
+ * The checks of design `client-ranges` — the backend's `CardioLogSpecification` ranges, rower cadence 10–60 —
+ * on one field's [text]; null when it passes or is empty.
+ */
+internal fun fieldError(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    text: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (text.isBlank()) return null
+    val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    val imperial = units == DistanceUnit.MI
+    return when (field) {
+        CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+            durationError(text)
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+            distanceError(text, if (rower) DistanceUnit.M else units)
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+            paceError(text, if (rower) ROWER_MAX_PACE else HOUR, CardioMetric.PACE.unitLabel(activity, units))
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+            speedError(text, if (imperial) "mph" else "km/h")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+            wholeIn(text, 0..20_000, if (imperial) "ft" else "m")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+            wholeIn(text, 30..250, "bpm")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+            wholeIn(text, if (rower) 10..60 else 1..250, if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+            wholeIn(text, 1..2500, "W")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+            wholeIn(text, 0..20_000, "kcal")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+            null
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private const val USE_A_NUMBER = "use a number"
+
+private fun wholeIn(
+    text: String,
+    range: IntRange,
+    unit: String,
+): String? {
+    val value = text.trim().toIntOrNull() ?: return "use a whole number"
+    return if (value in range) null else "between ${range.first} and ${range.last} $unit"
+}
+
+private fun durationError(text: String): String? {
+    val seconds = parseDuration(text) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+    return if (seconds in 1..MAX_DURATION) null else "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION s"
+}
+
+/** Above 0 and under 1000 km, in [unit]. */
+private fun distanceError(
+    text: String,
+    unit: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    val value = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return USE_A_NUMBER
+    val max = MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / unit.metres
+    return if (value > 0 && value < max) null else "between 0 and ${number((max * TEN).toLong() / TEN)} ${unit.name.lowercase()}"
+}
+
+private fun paceError(
+    text: String,
+    max: Int,
+    unit: String?,
+): String? {
+    val seconds = parsePace(text) ?: return "use m:ss"
+    return if (seconds in MIN_PACE..max) null else "between ${clock(MIN_PACE)} and ${clock(max)} $unit"
+}
+
+private fun speedError(
+    text: String,
+    unit: String,
+): String? {
+    val value = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return USE_A_NUMBER
+    return if (value in MIN_SPEED..MAX_SPEED) null else "between $MIN_SPEED and ${number(MAX_SPEED)} $unit"
+}

new file mode 100644

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (KAN-939 L1–L6): each field's typed [texts], [effort], [note] and the fields the user
+ * has [left]. Pure — the sheet debounces and draws it. A blank pace or speed is [derived], never sent
+ * (design `derived-never-sent`); the rower's distance is metres whatever [units].
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Every field's error; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val errors =
+            activity.logFields
+                .mapNotNull { field -> fieldError(field, text(field), activity, units)?.let { field to it } }
+                .toMap()
+        val avg = int(AVG_HEART_RATE)
+        val max = int(MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        return if (MAX_HEART_RATE !in errors && avg != null && max != null && max < avg) {
+            errors + (MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average")
+        } else {
+            errors
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Duration parses and nothing is in error — design `client-ranges`'s primary. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = parseDuration(text(DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /** AVG_PACE as `m:ss`, AVG_SPEED one decimal — when the field is blank and duration and distance are known. */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        val duration = parseDuration(text(DURATION))
+        return when (field) {
+            AVG_PACE -> CardioFormat.derivedPace(duration, distance(), null, activity, units)?.let(::clock)
+            AVG_SPEED -> CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(duration, distance(), null, units)?.let(::speedText)
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The typed fields and effort; never the note (the workout's own), never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parseDuration(text(DURATION)) ?: 0,
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = typed(AVG_PACE)?.let(::parsePace),
+            avgSpeed = typed(AVG_SPEED)?.toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = int(AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = int(MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = int(CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = int(ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = int(AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = int(AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    private fun typed(field: CardioLogField): String? = text(field).trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && field in activity.logFields }
+
+    private fun int(field: CardioLogField): Int? = typed(field)?.toIntOrNull()
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? =
+        typed(DISTANCE)?.toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { it > 0 }?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit(activity, units)) }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** A new log; Duration prefilled with whole seconds since [CardioWorkout.startedAt] when in progress (L5). */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val startedAt = workout.startedAt.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+            val elapsed = startedAt?.let { (now - it).inWholeSeconds.toInt() }?.takeIf { it > 0 }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(DURATION to durationText(it)) }.orEmpty(),
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text, effort from it, note from the workout; a derived value stays derived. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log ?: return CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val unit = distanceUnit(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit)
+            val texts =
+                mapOf(
+                    DURATION to durationText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    DISTANCE to log.distance?.let { number(it.value * it.unit.metres / unit.metres) },
+                    AVG_PACE to log.avgPaceSeconds?.let(::clock),
+                    AVG_SPEED to log.avgSpeed?.let(::number),
+                    AVG_HEART_RATE to log.avgHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    MAX_HEART_RATE to log.maxHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    CALORIES to log.calories?.toString(),
+                    ELEVATION_GAIN to log.elevationGain?.toString(),
+                    AVG_CADENCE to log.avgCadence?.toString(),
+                    AVG_POWER to log.avgPower?.toString(),
+                )
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts.mapNotNull { (field, text) -> text?.let { field to it } }.toMap(),
+                effort = log.effort,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun distanceUnit(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+) = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+
+/** A field of the log sheet (KAN-939 L1–L6); which ones an activity takes is [CardioActivity.logFields]. */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val HEART_TO_NOTE = listOf(AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+
+/** Design `log-fields-table`: L1 (outdoor run), L3 (outdoor ride), L4 (rower), and step-4's prose for the rest. */
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN) + HEART_TO_NOTE
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE) + HEART_TO_NOTE - AVG_CADENCE
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_SPEED, ELEVATION_GAIN) + HEART_TO_NOTE.withPowerAfterCadence()
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_SPEED) + HEART_TO_NOTE.withPowerAfterCadence()
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE) + HEART_TO_NOTE
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER) + HEART_TO_NOTE
+    }
+
+private fun List<CardioLogField>.withPowerAfterCadence() = flatMap { if (it == AVG_CADENCE) listOf(it, AVG_POWER) else listOf(it) }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Easy",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN) = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity), now)
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val tail = listOf(AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        assertEquals(listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN) + tail, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                DURATION,
+                DISTANCE,
+                AVG_SPEED,
+                ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                AVG_CADENCE,
+                AVG_POWER,
+                CALORIES,
+                EFFORT,
+                NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE, CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE) + tail, CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft().with(DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft().with(DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach {
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft().with(DURATION, it).errors()[DURATION], it)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val empty = draft().with(DISTANCE, "5")
+        assertNull(empty.errors()[DURATION])
+        assertFalse(empty.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(empty.with(DURATION, "30:00").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d = draft().with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d = draft().with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02").with(AVG_PACE, "6:30")
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE).with(DURATION, "1:12:40").with(DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(DURATION, "29:54").with(DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val d = draft().with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "148").with(MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", d.errors()[MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(d.errors()[AVG_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(AVG_CADENCE, "8").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = draft().with(DURATION, "45")
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.errors()[DURATION])
+        assertNull(d.shownError(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.left(DURATION).shownError(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00.000Z"))
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(started, now).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), now).text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 139,
+                maxHeartRate = 148,
+                calories = 512,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 24,
+                avgCadence = 170,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED, log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", d.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("139", d.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("148", d.text(MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("512", d.text(CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", d.text(ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("170", d.text(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(6, d.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.note)
+        assertEquals("", d.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val request =
+            draft()
+                .with(DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "150")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+                .toRequest()
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(durationSeconds = 1992, distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), avgHeartRate = 150, effort = 6),
+            request,
+        )
+    }
+}
